# Volyume plan builder: full map for independent review (2026-10-11)

This single file joins every section of `docs/audit/plan-builder-map-2026-10-11/` in reading order, followed by the cited research gathered on 10 October. Section boundaries are marked with horizontal rules.


---

<!-- source file: docs/audit/plan-builder-map-2026-10-11/07-BRIEF-FOR-INDEPENDENT-REVIEW.md -->

# 07 Brief for an independent review (paste this with the map)

You are reviewing the training-plan builder of Volyume, a live bodybuilding app (React Native, offline-first). Attached is a factual map of how it works today, produced from the code and from real plans generated by the code. The founder does not want the previous engineer's opinions; treat the map's descriptions as claims to check against the samples and measurements in it, and prefer the samples and measurements where they disagree with any prose.

Hard constraints you must design within (these are product law, not preferences):
- The coaching engine is deterministic: same inputs, same plan. No AI or randomness at runtime.
- Users have no fixed training days. Sessions run as a rotation, so the next session may be the next calendar day.
- Plans are built per category: general hypertrophy, open bodybuilding, men's physique, classic physique, bikini, wellness, figure, women's physique; 2 to 6 sessions a week; session length 30 to 90+ minutes; equipment from full gym down to bodyweight; up to three focus muscles ("bring up" picks); beginner to advanced.
- Founder rules already given: the standard routine of the category is always kept, and focus picks add on top; nothing is removed to fit the clock (rest is shortened first, then the real length is shown); men's physique trains legs once a week; injury risk from poor structure is unacceptable.

What to produce:
1. For each category and each of 2 to 6 sessions a week: the correct weekly structure as a mainstream evidence-based coach would write it (session names, muscles per session, order), with sources (federation criteria, peer-reviewed volume and frequency research, and named coaches' published programmes). State where the evidence is thin.
2. Per muscle and category: weekly set ranges for an intermediate (minimum to grow, typical, upper), how many sessions a week, and minimum hours between two sessions that train it directly, with sources. Include indirect load (for example chest pressing loading front delts and triceps) and how it should count.
3. Rules a plan must never break, written so a program can check them (for example: no muscle trained directly in two back-to-back sessions; no overhead pressing the day after chest pressing; at most N exercises and M sets a session; per-exercise set caps). Give the reasoning and sources for each.
4. What should win when goals conflict (weekly volume, recovery between sessions, session length, focus picks, category emphasis), as an ordered priority list with reasons.
5. A diagnosis of the current builder using the map's samples and measured issues: what is structurally wrong, which mechanisms cause it, and whether the right fix is to repair the current planner, return to the older generator described in the map, or design a new approach. Be specific about which parts of each are worth keeping.
6. A test plan: the checks that should run against every plan the app can produce before any release.

Format: plain, structured, sources inline. Separate what the evidence supports from your judgement.

# 00 Read first: a factual map of the Volyume plan builder (2026-10-11)

**Purpose.** This folder is a map of how training plans are built and changed in the Volyume app today, written for an independent reviewer. It describes what the code does. It does not recommend fixes; the reviewer is asked for those in 07. The founder does not trust the previous engineer's summaries, so the map is built to be checked.

**How it was produced.** The code read is `main` at commit `39a1929` (app version 2.13.0). Nothing in the app was changed to produce it.

| File | What it is | How it was made |
|---|---|---|
| 01-BUILD-PIPELINE.md | Every step from the app's call to the saved plan, with file:line for each claim, the priority order, a full worked trace and 16 observed problems | Read from the code; the trace was run on the real code and library |
| 02-AFTER-BUILD-AND-OLDER-GENERATOR.md | Everything that changes a plan after it is built (serving each week, check-ins, swaps, rebuilds, library plans) and the older generator, with real samples and 18 observed problems | Read from the code; samples run on the real code, including the real database save path |
| 03-REAL-SAMPLES.md | 42 complete plans from the live planner: every category at 3 to 6 days, plus variations | Machine-generated by tools/generate-samples.jest.js; nothing typed by hand |
| 04-MEASURED-ISSUES.md | Measured problems over 1,440 plans (every category, 2 to 6 days, 2 lengths, 6 equipment settings, 3 focus picks) | Machine-generated, same tool |
| 05-BEFORE-AND-AFTER.md | The same 1,440 plans built by three versions of the code: 3 Oct (older generator), 9 Oct (new planner) and now | Machine-generated by tools/measure-core.js and its runners |
| 06-CHANGE-HISTORY.md | Every planner commit since 4 Oct, and exactly what the 10 Oct changes did | git log and the diffs |
| 07-BRIEF-FOR-INDEPENDENT-REVIEW.md | The questions for the reviewer | Written by the lead |
| 08-RULEBOOK-FRAMEWORK.md | The decisions a rulebook must make, left blank for the founder's research | Written by the lead |
| tools/ | The generators, so every number can be re-produced | |

**Trust order.** Prefer the measurements (04, 05) and samples (03) over any prose, including this page. 01 and 02 cite file:line for every claim and mark anything unverified. The lead spot-checked these 01 and 02 claims against the code: the light and heavy split not recomputed after the reorder, the weekly rows written only for the planner's own muscles, the single front-delt exercise and its thin-equipment cap, readiness not gating the save, and the older generator's over-cap isolation sets.

**Related research** (cited, gathered 10 Oct): `docs/audit/plan-builder-science-2026-10-04/06-DIVISION-RESEARCH-MENS-PHYSIQUE-CLASSIC.md`, `07-DIVISION-RESEARCH-BIKINI-WELLNESS.md`, `08-DIVISION-RESEARCH-FIGURE-WOMENS-PHYSIQUE.md` and `09-DIVISION-RESEARCH-BODYBUILDING-GENERAL-SURVEY.md`. File 05 in that folder was a weak single pass and is superseded by 06 to 09.

## Timeline

- **Up to 3 October:** plans were built by the older generator (`src/lib/planEngine.js` `generatePlan`). The founder judged these plans good.
- **4 and 5 October:** a new planner (`src/lib/plan/`) was built around recovery and switched on at 09:22 on 5 October (`PLANNER_V2 = true` in `src/lib/plan/release.js`). Active plans are rebuilt by it at the person's next session.
- **10 October:** the lead made per-muscle and per-category set floors outrank session limits and recovery, rewrote every physique category's session lists, and changed men's physique to one leg day. Details in 06.

## How a plan is made today, in brief (details and line references in 01)

1. **Which code runs.** With the switch on and no style pool, the new planner builds the plan. With a style pool, the older generator still runs (01 section 1, 02 section 6).
2. **Inputs.** Days a week (2 to 6, a beginner at most 4), session length, equipment, goal or category, experience, phase, recovery rating and up to three focus muscles, plus the person's logged history and learned recovery when present (01 section 2).
3. **Exercises.** For each muscle, an ordered list of exercises the person's equipment allows, preferring exercises they have logged. Each compound credits half a set to its helper muscles. Front delts have exactly one exercise in every equipment setting (01 section 3).
4. **Roles and floors.** Each muscle is a focus muscle, a standard muscle or a maintenance muscle, with a weekly peak, a growth floor and, since 10 Oct, a per-category direct floor (01 section 4).
5. **Week structure.** Physique categories have exactly one structure per day count: a hand-written session list. General and bodybuilding choose among several general structures (01 section 5).
6. **Placing the sets.** Sixteen priorities apply in a fixed order (01 section 9). In short: the day count and the category's list come first, then caps per exercise and per session, then every trained muscle's first exercise, then the floors. The floors are placed past the session length and the 8-exercise and 25-set ceilings. After that come the structure search, session length and ceilings, growth above the floors, session order, the light and heavy split, and the recovery promise. The recovery promise is fixed by structure, then by volume, but only if no floor is lost; otherwise it is reported and the plan is saved anyway.
7. **The block.** Six weeks: week 1 starts well below the peak, the climb reaches the peak in week 5, and week 6 is the recovery week. A week server splits each week's sets across sessions and exercises (01 section 7).
8. **After the build.** Seventeen paths can change a plan. None of them checks which sessions sit next to each other, and few check recovery (02 section 7).

## Headline measurements (05)

| Measure, over 1,440 plans | 3 Oct | 9 Oct | Now |
|---|---|---|---|
| The planner's own recovery check fails | no such check | 33% | 80% |
| Same muscle trained directly in back-to-back sessions 24 h apart in the usual layout | 26% | 6% | 37% |
| Same exercise in two back-to-back sessions | 44% | 54% | 74% |
| A session over 8 exercises or 25 sets | 0% | 17% | 79% |
| A session more than 5 minutes past the chosen length | 56% | 28% | 80% |
| Average exercises per session | 5.5 | 5.9 | 9.3 |
| Average direct sets per week | 74.6 | 71.9 | 109.1 |

## Every observed problem, grouped (sources in brackets)

**Structure and recovery**
- Physique categories have one hand-written session list per day count, so the planner cannot choose a different structure when recovery fails (01 section 9 item 2).
- Men's physique 4 days now has three upper sessions that each list the same six upper muscles. Shoulders + Arms nearly copies Upper A (03 sample A; 05).
- On 9 Oct, men's physique 4 days had two identical back-and-delts sessions (05, founder's plan by version).
- The recovery check is not a gate: a failing plan is saved (01 section 9 item 11; `planner.js` chooseFamily).
- The light and heavy split is not recomputed when the order changes (01 P3).
- The rotation's clash term outweighs recovery about a thousand to one (01 P6).
- In the worked trace the recovery promise fails for four muscles and no fix is possible (01 P5).

**Floors outrank limits (since 10 Oct)**
- Session length does not limit any set placed by a floor (01 P1).
- The 8-exercise and 25-set ceilings are exceeded (01 P2; 02 P5; 04).
- Credits push muscles above their peak, so the "lower the peak" fix does nothing (01 P4).

**Exercise choice**
- Front delts have one exercise in every equipment setting, so the "thin equipment" bonus raises its cap to 6 sets (01 P7; lead check). A 6-set overhead press appears in samples (05).
- The same exercise appears in back-to-back sessions in 74% of plans (04).

**After the build**
- A planner plan loses its planner targets at the next block and whenever it is set active again (02 P1).
- Template rows for muscles the planner does not target stay in the block and leak into screens and swaps (01 P11; 02 P2).
- A strong check-in adds far less than its card says, and the card counts the plan's own climb (02 P3).
- The check-in opens exercises on manual plans but never on planner plans (02 P4).
- Different screens serve different weeks when the programme position lags the calendar (02 P10, traced from code, not run).
- The rebuild at the next session discards applied check-ins (02 P11).
- "Update your plan" with the block kept overwrites protected rows (02 P12).
- A permanent swap into another muscle is not held to the destination's limits (02 P13).
- A permanent swap keeps shortened rest as if the person set it (02 P14).
- The reintroduction ramp treats week 1 as the current week (02 P15).
- Plans without v2 facts are served by an uncapped multiplier (02 P17).
- Check-in later-week writes are outside the transaction (02 P18).

**Older generator**
- It writes exercises above the per-exercise cap, which the multiplier then raises further (02 P6).
- Its men's physique 4-day plan delivers no abs and drops listed muscles (02 P7).
- Its exercise ranking adds the raw pool index, contrary to its comment (02 P8).
- Screens describe the older generator's plan while the saved plan is the new planner's (02 P9).

**Inputs and data**
- The aggressive-cut peak reduction cannot be reached from a profile (01 P8).
- The ramp is skipped for a person with no recent training (01 P9).
- The strength-phase session-length estimate uses the wrong rest (01 P10).
- Focus picks are cut to three before duplicates are merged (01 P13).
- The plan's day count can differ from the profile's (01 P14).
- The style-pool test differs between save and generation (01 P15).
- The demonstrated-structure read is computed but not used (01 P16).
- Comments disagree with the code (01 P12; 02 P16).

**Process**
- The planner's tests use a fixture exercise list with no front-delt exercises, so they never exercised an overhead press. The lead's verification on 10 Oct used the same fixture.
- No test checks structure: back-to-back muscles, repeated exercises or near-copy sessions. The tests check sets, caps and floors.
- On 10 Oct the lead saw the recovery check fail for men's physique at 4 days and shipped the plan anyway.

# 05 Before and after: the same 1,440 plans built by three versions of the code

Generated 2026-10-11. Three copies of the repository were measured with one shared measurement script (`tools/measure-core.js`), so every number below is computed the same way for each version. Nothing was edited in any version.

| Version | Commit | What builds plans in the app |
|---|---|---|
| 3 Oct | `a7c4333` (2026-10-03 18:06) | The older generator, `src/lib/planEngine.js` `generatePlan`. The new planner did not exist yet. |
| 9 Oct | `ddcf0a5` (2026-10-09 19:26) | The new planner, `src/lib/plan/planner.js` `buildPlan`, switched on 2026-10-05 09:22 (`4907cee`, `PLANNER_V2 = true`). This is the state before the lead's changes of 10 October. |
| Now | `39a1929` (main, version 2.13.0) | The new planner after the lead's 10 October changes (see 06-CHANGE-HISTORY.md). |

Inputs, the same for every version: 8 categories (mens_physique, classic_physique, bodybuilding, bikini, wellness, figure, womens_physique, general) x 2 to 6 days x 45 and 75 minutes x 6 equipment settings (full_gym, machines_cables, dumbbells_only, home_gym, barbell_plates, bodyweight) x 3 focus picks (none; chest; glutes + side delts + chest), intermediate. The new planner was called the way the app calls it, with the real seeded exercise library (`src/lib/__tests__/campaign16.helpers.js` LIBRARY, built from `src/lib/exerciseCorpus` as the seed builds it). The older generator was called the way the app called it before 5 October (`generatePlan` with its built-in exercise pool), with focus picks passed as its weak-point labels.

Read the measures with these limits:

- **Back-to-back** means two sessions next to each other in the served order, including the last session back to the first. The app does not fix training days, so any back-to-back pair may be trained on consecutive days. At 2 and 3 days a week, the same muscles in neighbouring sessions is ordinary full-body training, because those sessions are 48 to 96 hours apart in the usual layout. The **24 h apart** rows are the meaningful recovery measure.
- **Session minutes** for the older generator are its own estimate (`estimatedDurationMinutes`). For the new planner they are the planner's estimate at the peak week (`v2.sessionMinutesAtPeak`). The two formulas differ, so compare the over-length rows within a version more than across versions.
- **Readiness check** is the new planner's own recovery simulation (`v2.readiness.passes`). The older generator has no such check.
- **Below today's category floor** measures every version against the per-category floors written on 10 October (`src/lib/plan/divisionStandard.js`, else `src/lib/plan/science.js` `STANDARD_DIRECT_FLOOR`). Those floors did not exist on 3 or 9 October, so this row shows how far the earlier versions sat from them, not a defect those versions were built to avoid.
- **Front delts pressed** means an overhead press, or a press that credits the front delts (new planner: catalogue credits; older generator: the pool's secondary muscles).

## The founder's plan, built by each version

Men's physique, 60 minutes, full gym, intermediate. New-planner sets are shown as week 1 / peak week; the older generator shows its prescribed sets. Session order is the served order.

#### 3 Oct older generator: men's physique, 4 days, 60 min, full gym, focus side delts + biceps

- **Back + Delts (Width)** (about 68 min): Incline Dumbbell Curl 3x10-20; Barbell Curl 3x10-20; Lat Pulldown (Wide Grip) 3x8-12; Barbell Row (Bent Over) 3x6-10; Cable Lateral Raise 3x10-20; Dumbbell Lateral Raise 4x10-20; Face Pull 3x10-20
- **Lower + Abs** (about 70 min): Bulgarian Split Squat 3x8-12; Leg Extension 3x10-20; Romanian Deadlift (Barbell) 3x6-10; Lying Leg Curl 3x8-15; Barbell Hip Thrust 3x8-12; Standing Calf Raise (Machine) 3x10-20; Cable Crunch 3x10-20
- **Chest + Arms** (about 55 min): Incline Barbell Bench Press 4x6-10; Barbell Bench Press 3x6-10; EZ Bar Skull Crusher 3x10-20; Spider Curl 3x10-20; EZ Bar Curl 3x10-20
- **Back + Delts (Thickness)** (about 64 min): Lat Pulldown (Close Grip) 4x8-12; T-Bar Row 3x6-10; Reverse Pec Deck 3x10-20; Machine Lateral Raise 3x10-20; Leaning Lateral Raise 4x10-20; Barbell Shrug 3x10-20

#### 3 Oct older generator: men's physique, 4 days, 60 min, full gym, no focus

- **Back + Delts (Width)** (about 58 min): Lat Pulldown (Wide Grip) 4x8-12; Barbell Row (Bent Over) 4x6-10; Cable Lateral Raise 3x10-20; Dumbbell Lateral Raise 3x10-20; Face Pull 3x10-20

# 04 Measured issues across every input combination (generated 2026-10-11)

Built 1440 plans with the live planner and the real exercise library: 8 categories x 2 to 6 days x 45 and 75 minutes x 6 equipment settings x 3 focus picks (none; chest; glutes + side delts + chest), intermediate, first block. Generator: tools/generate-samples.jest.js. Each measure is computed from the plan the app would save; definitions are under the table.

Plans that failed to build: 0.

## All plans

| Measure | Plans affected |
|---|---|
| The planner's own readiness check fails | 1158 of 1440 (80%) |
| At least one back-to-back pair trains the same muscle directly (2+ sets in both) | 1168 of 1440 (81%) |
| ...and that pair is 24 h apart in the app's usual layout | 532 of 1440 (37%) |
| The same exercise appears in two back-to-back sessions | 1062 of 1440 (74%) |
| The front delts are pressed in two back-to-back sessions | 661 of 1440 (46%) |
| An overhead press appears in 2 or more sessions | 360 of 1440 (25%) |
| A session runs more than 5 min past the chosen length | 1147 of 1440 (80%) |
| A session holds more than 8 exercises or 25 sets | 1142 of 1440 (79%) |
| A growth muscle sits below its category's direct floor | 544 of 1440 (38%) |

## By category

| Category | Plans | Readiness fails | Back-to-back same muscle | ...24 h apart | Front delts pressed back to back | Over length | Over 8 ex / 25 sets | Longest session min | Lower-body sessions per week (min-max) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| mens_physique | 180 | 144 | 180 | 108 | 138 | 139 | 134 | 175 | 1-2 |
| classic_physique | 180 | 144 | 103 | 31 | 72 | 144 | 128 | 187 | 2-3 |
| bodybuilding | 180 | 143 | 106 | 16 | 104 | 164 | 174 | 221 | 1-3 |
| bikini | 180 | 136 | 180 | 108 | 36 | 133 | 132 | 166 | 2-3 |
| wellness | 180 | 160 | 180 | 108 | 36 | 143 | 166 | 177 | 2-4 |
| figure | 180 | 145 | 144 | 60 | 75 | 140 | 132 | 185 | 2-2 |
| womens_physique | 180 | 146 | 144 | 60 | 84 | 142 | 143 | 196 | 2-3 |
| general | 180 | 140 | 131 | 41 | 116 | 142 | 133 | 192 | 1-3 |

## By days per week

| Days | Plans | Readiness fails | Back-to-back same muscle | ...24 h apart | Over length | Longest session min |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2 | 288 | 160 | 288 | 0 | 288 | 221 |
| 3 | 288 | 236 | 288 | 0 | 277 | 176 |
| 4 | 288 | 216 | 128 | 127 | 230 | 132 |
| 5 | 288 | 284 | 240 | 208 | 186 | 108 |
| 6 | 288 | 262 | 224 | 197 | 166 | 107 |

## By equipment

| Equipment | Plans | Readiness fails | Back-to-back same muscle | Over length | Below floor |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| full_gym | 240 | 203 | 187 | 214 | 0 |
| machines_cables | 240 | 199 | 191 | 196 | 4 |
| dumbbells_only | 240 | 177 | 201 | 179 | 30 |
| home_gym | 240 | 177 | 201 | 185 | 30 |
| barbell_plates | 240 | 203 | 199 | 207 | 240 |
| bodyweight | 240 | 199 | 189 | 166 | 240 |

## Which muscles are trained directly in back-to-back sessions, and how often

| Muscle | Plans where it is trained directly in two back-to-back sessions |
|---|---|
| calves | 739 of 1440 |
| abs | 721 of 1440 |
| glutes | 684 of 1440 |
| hamstrings | 615 of 1440 |
| chest | 583 of 1440 |
| triceps | 562 of 1440 |
| side_delts | 558 of 1440 |
| back | 537 of 1440 |
| biceps | 514 of 1440 |
| quads | 506 of 1440 |
| rear_delts | 371 of 1440 |
| front_delts | 196 of 1440 |
| traps | 137 of 1440 |
| adductors | 105 of 1440 |
| forearms | 91 of 1440 |

## Definitions

- Back-to-back: two sessions next to each other in the served order, including the last session to the first. The app does not fix training days, so any such pair can be trained on consecutive days.
- Trained directly: the muscle has 2 or more peak-week sets on its own exercises in that session.
- Front delts pressed: the session has an overhead press (a compound exercise whose muscle is front delts) or a press whose catalogue credits include the front delts.
- Readiness check: `v2.readiness.passes` from the planner, which simulates recovery at the app's usual spacing (src/lib/plan/readiness.js).
- Over length: `v2.overTime`, more than 5 minutes past the chosen session length after the rest ladder. Direct floor: src/lib/plan/divisionStandard.js, else src/lib/plan/science.js STANDARD_DIRECT_FLOOR.

# 06 Change history of the planner, 4 to 10 October 2026

Every commit since 2026-10-04 that touched `src/lib/plan/`, `src/lib/planEngine.js`, `src/lib/planAutoGen.js`, `src/lib/exercise/continuity.js` or `src/lib/coachApply.js` (board and handover commits left out). Produced by `git log`; the subjects are the commit subjects as written at the time.

## Timeline

- **Up to 2026-10-03:** plans were built by the older generator, `src/lib/planEngine.js` `generatePlan`.
- **2026-10-04:** register D219 (founder order: rebuild the plan builder around recovery). The new planner was built behind a switch that was off.
- **2026-10-05 09:22, `4907cee`:** switch on (`src/lib/plan/release.js` `PLANNER_V2 = true`). From here every new plan, and every plan rebuilt at a person's next session, comes from the new planner. The older generator still runs when a style pool is present (see 02).
- **2026-10-05 to 2026-10-09:** D219 lanes, review fixes and founder answers.
- **2026-10-10:** the lead's changes, in reaction to the founder's report that plans dropped standard muscles (chest at one or two exercises, legs held low in men's physique). Five decisions were recorded in the register, `docs/ux-world-class-audit-2026-07-09/DECISIONS-2026-07-09.md`, under the D219 addenda dated 2026-10-10 (unnumbered, 2, 3 and 4). Each is summarised below with its effect as measured in 05.

## Every commit

| Commit | When | Subject |
|---|---|---|
| dafc86f | 2026-10-04 12:32 | D219 lane A1: the plan's science table, the role-aware bands and prescribe() |
| ea78ee6 | 2026-10-04 14:32 | D219 lane B2: the new plan builder, not yet wired into the app |
| 301858a | 2026-10-04 14:45 | Planner: judge structures by what their sessions hold, not the clock |
| 748f3fc | 2026-10-04 14:58 | Planner: a focus muscle's sets are never cut (founder rule 2026-10-04) |
| 6cab20d | 2026-10-04 15:02 | Planner: no session mixes the halves of the body |
| 5876a16 | 2026-10-04 15:07 | Planner: focus sets may pass the D45 session ceilings (founder 2026-10-04) |
| 00177a4 | 2026-10-04 15:09 | A focus muscle's isolation exercise may take 4 sets (founder 2026-10-04) |
| 3803513 | 2026-10-04 15:15 | D219 lane S1: the plan's facts stored, and every week served through prescribe() |
| 99e15ee | 2026-10-04 15:20 | D219 lanes B1 and B6: the standard catalogue, and new plans from the new planner (switch off) |
| 69407e0 | 2026-10-04 16:02 | D219 lane B7: curated credits, slot facts, preview, continuity, the Q5 ladder, typed counts |
| 4907cee | 2026-10-05 09:22 | D219: new plans are built by the new planner (PLANNER_V2 on) |
| d86eb5f | 2026-10-05 12:03 | D219 lane A3: check-ins placed set by set (design 4.10) |
| 9b11c87 | 2026-10-05 12:34 | D219 lane B5: every plan fact shown with its reason (design 6) |
| 8763220 | 2026-10-05 12:35 | Store a plan's session length facts, so a long session says so |
| 839eace | 2026-10-05 13:32 | D219 lane C1a: the planner's fixed-structure mode (register build ruling 5) |
| 49cc47e | 2026-10-05 13:58 | D219 lane R3: the session's +1 under the caps, the learner's reach, the learned factor, the forecast |
| cdd27e9 | 2026-10-05 14:42 | D219 review fixes: served order, exercise floors, full-body big five, recovery-safe maximum |
| 62d7d6a | 2026-10-05 14:45 | Planner: letter each kind of session in the order it is trained |
| c4aa58c | 2026-10-05 14:54 | D219: swaps keep their caps and move their sets; plans people follow are rebuilt at the next session |
| 4403e3e | 2026-10-05 15:02 | D219 review fixes: check-ins stop at the recovery-safe maximum; the +1 top; the ramp reads the person's history |
| 8ddf8ea | 2026-10-05 15:10 | D219: the recovery-safe maxima hold together; check-ins on such a plan stay in its exercises |
| 7012d17 | 2026-10-05 17:09 | Planner: big muscles first when time is short |
| d37493f | 2026-10-05 18:04 | Planner: a slower recoverer's lower session cap (design 4.4) |
| f2ba8bb | 2026-10-10 04:51 | Planner: the standard floor, every muscle's standard kept in every goal (WIP checkpoint) |
| 7f3e10e | 2026-10-10 04:52 | Planner: a session's half is the half most of its muscles are in |
| d5f8757 | 2026-10-10 05:14 | Planner: the standard floor's week server, tests and matrix fixes |
| 4a28773 | 2026-10-10 05:18 | Planner: trim rest on a graded ladder before a session is allowed to run over |
| d411df7 | 2026-10-10 05:21 | Planner: the old generator's suites pin the standard, not the cut |
| 70345ba | 2026-10-10 06:54 | Planner: the standard per category, from the cited research |
| 0dac0bb | 2026-10-10 06:58 | Planner: figure's 3-day week trains triceps twice; the old generator's pins read the category lists |
| 9398ce9 | 2026-10-10 07:20 | Legacy generator: a division's session names are identity, never relettered by position |
| 070db3f | 2026-10-10 09:01 | Men's physique: one leg day a week at every day count; a muscle's own day is its heavy session |
| 4be8f2e | 2026-10-10 09:08 | Planner: a week's muscles spread by running session load |

## What each 10 October commit changed, in the code

| Commit | Change | Files |
|---|---|---|
| `f2ba8bb` | Every growth muscle made a standard role in every goal; a division overlay below 1.0 no longer made a muscle maintenance. A direct floor per muscle (`STANDARD_DIRECT_FLOOR`) added and placed on the muscle's own exercises after the fractional floors, ignoring the session length and the D45 ceilings of 8 exercises and 25 sets (`ignoreTime: true`). Floors for big muscles placed past the clock. A division list stopped limiting where a muscle trains: a muscle named in fewer sessions than its standard needs may train in any session of its body half. The big five muscles trained twice from 3 sessions. Readiness fixes, light and heavy splits and the session-count search changed so that none may leave a muscle below its direct floor; the plan's own per-muscle fractional cap may be raised to place the floor. | roles.js, science.js, families.js, planner.js, allocate.js, planEngine.js (DIVISION_MATRIX) |
| `7f3e10e` | A session counts as one body half by the majority of its muscles. | families.js |
| `d5f8757` | Week server: a set can move off an exercise that credits a muscle already over its cap; a crediting set can move to another session. Session balancing keeps the allocator's split where refilling would break a cap. Equally spaced sessions prefer dedicated ones. Continuity applies a reviewed rep range to every slot of the exercise. Tests re-pinned. | prescribe.js, planner.js, families.js, continuity.js, tests |
| `4a28773` | Rest ladder: a session past the chosen length shortens rest step by step (small-muscle isolation to 60 s, all isolation to 60 s, machines to 90 s, moderate compounds to 120 s, heavy compounds to 150 s) and then reports what is still over. No exercise or set is removed for time. | allocate.js, explain.js |
| `d411df7` | Older generator's tests re-pinned to the new lists. | tests, planEngine.js |
| `70345ba` | Per-category standard: `src/lib/plan/divisionStandard.js` (direct floors and sessions per week per muscle per category, each with a cited source line from research files 06 to 09 in `docs/audit/plan-builder-science-2026-10-04/`). DIVISION_MATRIX rewritten for every division and day count. A division list became authoritative for where a muscle trains (it trains in every session the list names). A de-emphasised muscle held at its floor with weight 0. A muscle trained once a week opens with its compounds. Abs may fall back to any session. | divisionStandard.js, roles.js, planner.js, allocate.js, families.js, planEngine.js |
| `0dac0bb` | Figure 3-day list: triceps added to a second session. Older generator: a muscle trained in two sessions rotates its roles by its own visit count. | planEngine.js, tests |
| `9398ce9` | Older generator: a division's session names are kept as authored after recovery reordering, instead of relettered by position. | planEngine.js |
| `070db3f` | Men's physique: one lower-body session at every day count; 4-day list Upper A (Width), Legs + Abs, Upper B (Detail), Shoulders + Arms; 6-day list push, pull, legs, push, pull, shoulders and arms. In a division week, the session whose list names a muscle first becomes its heavy session for the light and heavy split. | divisionStandard.js, planEngine.js, planner.js |
| `4be8f2e` | Each muscle's sessions chosen by the running number of muscles already placed in each session (spreads 3-day full-body weeks). | planner.js |

## The 10 October rulings and their measured effect

Figures from 05-BEFORE-AND-AFTER.md, all 1,440 combinations, 9 Oct against now:

- The planner's own readiness check fails in 33% of plans on 9 Oct and 80% now.
- Back-to-back sessions 24 h apart that train the same muscle directly: 6% on 9 Oct, 37% now.
- Sessions over 8 exercises or 25 sets: 17% on 9 Oct, 79% now.
- Sessions more than 5 minutes past the chosen length: 28% on 9 Oct, 80% now.
- Average weekly direct sets: 71.9 on 9 Oct, 109.1 now. Average exercises per session: 5.9 on 9 Oct, 9.3 now.
- Plans with a growth muscle below the 10 October floors: about 100% on 9 Oct, 38% now. Raising these floors was the purpose of the changes; the cost is the rows above.

The mechanism, from the code: on 10 October the direct floors were made to outrank the session ceilings, the session length and the readiness check (`allocate.js` floor steps with `ignoreTime: true`; `planner.js` `keepsFocusFloor` rejecting light caps that leave a muscle below its direct floor; the readiness-fix acceptance test requiring every muscle to keep its direct floor; the final standard guard in `evaluateFamily` that drops light caps and raises fractional caps until the floors are placed). When the floors and recovery could not both be met, the floors won and the readiness check reported a failure.

# Plan builder map, part 1: the build pipeline of the live planner

Written 2026-10-11 for an independent review. Read against the working tree at
commit `39a1929f77f36a78384c747a4923cb842cf8a82d` (branch `main`, read from
`.git/HEAD`). Every claim cites `file:line`. Paths are relative to the repo
root; `planner.js`, `allocate.js` and the other bare names are in
`src/lib/plan/` unless a path is given.

Conventions used here:

- "Observed" means the code was executed (section 10 and section 11 say how)
  or read to the end of the function that consumes the value. "UNVERIFIED"
  means the claim rests on code not read to the end, with the reason stated.
- Where a comment and the code disagree, the code is described and the
  disagreement is listed in section 11.
- Sets: "direct" sets are an exercise's own sets for its muscle; "fractional"
  sets add 0.5 of a set to each muscle an exercise credits
  (`COUNTING.synergistCredit = 0.5`, `science.js:77-79`; the credits
  themselves are the catalogue's, section 3.4).
- A session order is written as the session indexes in the order trained,
  for example `0132` means s0, s1, s3, s2.

Out of scope for this part: how a saved plan is served week by week at run
time (check-ins, `checkinPlacement.js`, `coachApply.js`), the legacy generator
`planEngine.generatePlan` beyond the point where it is called, and the
internals of `exercise/continuity.js applyContinuity`, which this part did not
read (section 1.7 says what that leaves unverified).

---

## 0. The pipeline at a glance

In execution order, for a new plan with the release switch on and no style
pool:

1. `generateAndSavePlan` builds the inputs from the profile
   (`buildPlanInputs`, `planAutoGen.js:111-145`), loads the library, the
   person's exercise intent and the active plan's style tag
   (`planAutoGen.js:990-1029`).
2. It hands over to `saveWithPlannerV2` (`planAutoGen.js:1037-1043`), which
   asks continuity which of the person's current exercises are kept
   (`plannerV2KeptExercises`, `planAutoGen.js:1583-1613`).
3. `buildPlanWithPlannerV2` (`planAutoGen.js:1765-1816`) resolves the
   standard catalogue for the person's kit (`catalogue.js:685-743`), puts the
   kept exercises into it (`plannerV2KeptChoices`, `planAutoGen.js:1501-1569`),
   reads history (first block, learned factor, own gaps, logged weekly sets)
   and calls `buildPlan` (`planner.js:108-161`).
4. `buildPlan` picks the candidate week structures (a division's own list,
   or the general families), assigns every muscle a role (`roles.js:80-157`),
   evaluates every structure (`evaluateFamily`, `planner.js:399-1003`),
   chooses one (`chooseFamily`, `planner.js:1318-1336`), optionally re-orders
   it on the person's own recovery clocks (`personalise`,
   `planner.js:263-285`), computes each muscle's recovery-safe maximum
   (`planner.js:178-255`) and shapes the output (`toPlan`,
   `planner.js:1363-1453`).
5. Inside `evaluateFamily`: sessions a week per muscle, the peak week's sets
   (`allocatePeakWeek`, `allocate.js:133-548`), a session-count search, the
   rotation order (`rotation.js`), the light and heavy split, the six-week
   block (`targets.js`, `prescribe.js`), the readiness simulation
   (`readiness.js`), readiness fixes, the ramp, the slower-recoverer cap, the
   standard-floor guard and the family's scores.
6. Back in `saveWithPlannerV2`: one continuity and resolution pass
   (`resolvePlannerV2Plan`, `planAutoGen.js:1632-1657`), then one transaction
   writing the programme, routines, routine exercises and plan facts
   (`planAutoGen.js:1954-2003`), then block activation, the weekly
   planned-volume rows (`writePlannerV2WeeklyRows`,
   `planAutoGen.js:1827-1867`) and archiving of other plans.
7. Any planner failure, an empty catalogue or a plan that resolves to nothing
   (not because of the person's exclusions) returns `null`, and the legacy
   generator `planEngine.generatePlan` builds the plan instead
   (`planAutoGen.js:1042-1047`).

---

## 1. Entry points (`src/lib/planAutoGen.js`)

### 1.1 The release switch

- `PLANNER_V2 = true` (`src/lib/plan/release.js:22`). The only importer in
  `src/` outside tests is `planAutoGen.js:47` (grep for `plan/release`).
- It is read at exactly two places: `generateAndSavePlan`
  (`planAutoGen.js:1037`) and `generatePlanDryRun` (`planAutoGen.js:2324`).
  `planGeneratedRebuildV2` and `planFixedRebuildV2` call the new planner
  without reading the switch (`planAutoGen.js:2087-2183`).

### 1.2 Which generator runs

| Case | Generator | Code |
|---|---|---|
| Switch on, active plan's tags carry no `style:` key, or a key `stylePoolFor` does not know | New planner (`saveWithPlannerV2`) | `planAutoGen.js:1024-1043`; `stylePoolFor` returns null for an unknown key (`exercise/stylePools.js:189-193`) |
| Switch on, active plan has a known style key (kettlebell or circuit library template) | Legacy `generatePlan` with `stylePool` | `planAutoGen.js:1029`, `1045-1061` |
| New planner returns `null` (planner threw, no workouts, no `v2`, the catalogue had no row for the kit, or nothing resolved and no slot was blocked by the person's exclusions) | Legacy `generatePlan` | `planAutoGen.js:1914-1919`, `1929-1939`, `1765-1779` |
| New planner resolves nothing because the person's exclusions blocked every slot | Neither: returns `{ ok: false, error: 'plan_blocked_by_exclusions', ... }` | `planAutoGen.js:1929-1937` |

`planGeneratedRebuildV2` treats ANY style key as `style_plan` (refuses)
(`planAutoGen.js:2093-2097`), whereas the save path only diverts when the key
resolves to a pool. Listed in section 11 (P15).

### 1.3 `generateAndSavePlan(userId, profile, { ledger, allowLearnedCarry, continuityProposal, keepBlock })`

`planAutoGen.js:975-1238`. Callers found by grep: `startWithPlan.js:147`,
`PlanUpdateScreen.js:361` (passes `keepBlock`), `ProOnboardingScreen.js:2022`
and `:2080`, `PlansScreen.js:767` (passes `ledger: seedRanges`,
`allowLearnedCarry: true`, `continuityProposal`).

Reads, in order:

1. `buildPlanInputs(profile)`; null means `{ ok: false, error: 'Profile incomplete' }` (`:979-980`).
2. `getAllExercises()` (`:990-993`), failure tolerated as `[]`.
3. `loadGenerationIntent(userId)` (`:999`, defined `:358-369`): `getActiveBlock` then `loadExerciseIntentState`; any failure returns `null`.
4. `filterLibraryForGeneration(allExercises, intentState)` (`:1000`); returns `{ library, dropped, ... }`; with no intents and no capability rules it returns the same array (`exercise/generation.js:148-157`).
5. `reviewedReplacementIds(continuityProposal)` (`:1001`, defined `:653-660`): ids whose reviewed verdict is `replace` or `remove_or_redistribute`; `libraryForReviewedProposal` removes them (`:670-673`). The result is `generationLibrary`.
6. `readDemonstratedStructure(userId, inputs.daysPerWeek)` (`:1012-1015`). This result is passed only to the legacy generator (`:1049`); the new planner never receives it, and the preview reports `structureMemory: null` for the new planner (`:2247`).
7. `getActivePlan(userId)` for the style tag (`:1024-1029`).

Then, with the switch on and no pool, `saveWithPlannerV2` (`:1889-2060`, section 8). If it returns a result, that is returned (`:1042`). Otherwise the legacy path runs from `:1045`.

### 1.4 `generatePlanDryRun(userId, profile, { continuityProposal })`

`planAutoGen.js:2285-2423`. Callers: `startWithPlan.js:104`,
`PlanUpdateScreen.js:228`, `PlansScreen.js:692`. Same reads as 1.3 steps 1 to
7 (`:2287-2319`). With the switch on and no pool it calls
`previewWithPlannerV2` (`:2195-2263`), which runs the same kept-exercise pass,
`buildPlanWithPlannerV2` and `resolvePlannerV2Plan` as the save and returns
`{ ok: true, plan: { ...plan, workouts }, sessionLengthMinutes,
structureMemory: null, continuity, partial?, missedCount?, missedExercises? }`
plus the blocked-slot fields (`:2241-2262`). It writes nothing. It returns
`null`, and so falls back to the legacy preview, in the same cases as the save
(`:2217-2239`).

### 1.5 `planGeneratedRebuildV2({ userId, profile, daysPerWeek, replaceIds })`

`planAutoGen.js:2087-2134`. Caller: `planRebuild.js:332`, with
`daysPerWeek: routines.length` of the plan being rebuilt and
`replaceIds` = generator picks outside the catalogue (`planRebuild.js:329-334`).

1. `inputs = { ...buildPlanInputs(profile), daysPerWeek }` (`:2089-2091`).
2. `plannerV2SessionCount(inputs) !== daysPerWeek` returns `days_not_kept` (`:2092`). `plannerV2SessionCount` is `max(2, min(6, round(daysPerWeek || 3)))`, capped at 4 for a beginner (`:1664-1668`), the same rule as `planner.js:311-316`.
3. Any `style:` tag on the active plan returns `style_plan` (`:2093-2097`).
4. Library, intent, filter as 1.3; `continuityProposal` is always `null` here (`:2099-2102`).
5. Kept exercises (`plannerV2KeptExercises`) minus `replaceIds` (`:2107-2111`).
6. `buildPlanWithPlannerV2` (`:2112`); null returns `no_catalogue`; no workouts or no `v2` returns `no_plan`; a workout count different from `daysPerWeek` returns `days_not_kept` (`:2113-2115`).
7. `resolvePlannerV2Plan` with incumbents narrowed by `replaceIds` (`:2116-2126`); nothing resolved returns `nothing_resolved`; a resolved workout count different from `daysPerWeek` returns `days_not_kept` (`:2127-2128`).
8. Returns `{ ok: true, inputs, plan, resolved, planName }` (`:2129-2130`). It never writes; the writer is `database.rebuildPlanKeepingBlockV2`, called from `planRebuild.js` (not traced here).

### 1.6 `planFixedRebuildV2({ userId, profile, fixedSessions })`

`planAutoGen.js:2152-2183`. Caller: `planRebuild.js:397`, for a library, kit
or manual plan. It calls `buildPlan` directly with `fixedSessions` and
`daysPerWeek: sessions.length` (`:2162-2178`); `choices` and `loggedWeekly`
are not passed. In fixed mode the planner ignores `choices` and `daysPerWeek`
(`planner.js:98-106`, `109-110`) and skips the ramp (`planner.js:893`).
Returns `{ ok: true, inputs, plan }` or `{ ok: false, reason }`.

### 1.7 Helpers shared by the new-planner paths

| Helper | Lines | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| `plannerV2KeptExercises` | `1583-1613` | Loads the current plan's exercises (`loadIncumbentSlots`, `452-482`), de-duplicated by id, offers them to `withContinuity` (`685-760`) as one workout, keeps those whose decision is `SLOT_OUTCOME.RETAINED`, returns their library rows in plan order. Any failure returns `[]`. |
| `buildPlanWithPlannerV2` | `1765-1816` | Catalogue, kept choices, focus muscles, history, `buildPlan`, legacy-shaped summary. Detailed in section 2. |
| `plannerV2LoggedNames` | `1433-1441` | `getRecentlyUsedExerciseIds(userId, 400)` mapped to names. |
| `plannerV2FirstBlock` | `1448-1457` | `true` unless some mesocycle's `blockCompletionState` is `COMPLETED`; a read failure answers `true`. |
| `plannerV2Personalisation` | `1718-1751` | Reads the active plan's routines (in their stored order) and its facts' `builtFactor`, then `recovery/load.js loadPlanPersonalisation` (`load.js:515-561`). Returns `{ learnedFactor, ownGaps }`, each `null` on any failure. The learned factor is `null` unless the recovery learner has a personal reading and is not paused; when paused it is the old plan's `builtFactor` (`load.js:522-534`). `ownGaps` comes from `ownGapsFromHistory` over the last `ROTATION.ownGapsWindowWeeks` (8) weeks (`load.js:540-555`); its internals were not read (UNVERIFIED beyond that). |
| `plannerV2LoggedWeekly` | `1698-1716` | `getWeeklyVolumeByMuscle(userId, 4)`; keeps weeks with any positive volume; averages each muscle over those weeks; `null` when no week trained or on failure. |
| `resolvePlannerV2Plan` | `1632-1657` | Shapes workouts for writing (`plannerV2WorkoutsForWrite`, `1296-1325`), runs `withContinuity`, then `resolvePlanAgainstLibrary` (`772-833`), then `orderSamePositionContiguously` (`914-929`). |

`withContinuity` calls `applyContinuity` (`exercise/continuity.js`), which can
substitute a retained incumbent into a generated slot (`planAutoGen.js:742-755`).
This part did not read `applyContinuity`; whether a substituted exercise keeps
the planner's `muscle`, `kind` and `credits` fields on the row written to the
facts (`planAutoGen.js:1983-1988`) is UNVERIFIED.

`resolvePlanAgainstLibrary` drops an exercise that does not resolve to a
library row (`missedCount`) or that the person's intent blocks
(`blockedSlots`) (`planAutoGen.js:788-817`). The planner's facts (targets,
shares, caps) were computed before this pass and are written as computed
(`planAutoGen.js:1997-2001`); a dropped exercise is not re-planned. In the new
planner's path the catalogue only chooses from `generationLibrary`, which the
intent filter has already narrowed, so this should be rare; how often it occurs
is UNVERIFIED.

---

## 2. `buildPlanInputs` and what reaches the planner

### 2.1 `buildPlanInputs(profile)` (`planAutoGen.js:111-145`)

1. No `profile.trainingGoal`: returns `null` (`:112`).
2. `migrateProfileGoals(profile)` (`coachingGoals.js:402-430`): `general_hypertrophy` becomes `general`; `strength_hypertrophy` becomes `general` with phase `strength_size` when the phase was `bulk`, `lean_gain` or empty; `weak_point_spec` becomes `general` with phase `weak_point` under the same condition; every other goal is unchanged.

| Output field | Source | Default | Line |
|---|---|---|---|
| `experience` | `experience` | `'intermediate'` | `:123` |
| `daysPerWeek` | `daysPerWeek` | `DEFAULT_DAYS_PER_WEEK = 4` (`:99`) | `:124` (not clamped here) |
| `sessionLengthMinutes` | `sessionLengthMinutes` | `60` | `:125` |
| `equipment` | `equipment` | `'full_gym'` | `:126` |
| `goal` | `trainingGoal` (migrated) | none | `:127` |
| `phase` | `trainingPhase` (migrated) | `'maintain'` | `:121`, `:132` |
| `weakPoints` | `planWeakPoints` | `[]` | `:133` |
| `recoveryRating` | `recoveryRating` | `'average'` | `:134` |
| `nutritionPhase` | `phaseToNutritionKey(phase)` | `'maintain'` for an unknown phase | `:135`; `coachingGoals.js:306-308` |
| `age` | `ageFromDateOfBirth(dateOfBirth)` | `null` | `:143` |

`phaseToNutritionKey` maps (`coachingGoals.js:229-299`): `lean_gain` to
`lean_gain`, `bulk` to `build`, `strength_size` to `build`, `weak_point` to
`lean_gain`, `cut` to `mild_cut`, `recomp` to `recomp`, `maintain` to
`maintain`. Observed by execution (section 11, P8).

### 2.2 What `buildPlanWithPlannerV2` passes to `buildPlan` (`planAutoGen.js:1786-1809`)

| `buildPlan` input | Value | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `daysPerWeek` | `inputs.daysPerWeek` | clamped inside the planner (2.3) |
| `sessionLengthMinutes` | `inputs.sessionLengthMinutes` | |
| `equipment` | `inputs.equipment` | the planner uses it only for transition time (`allocate.js:53-55`, `228`); the catalogue uses it as the kit (`planAutoGen.js:1774-1778`) |
| `goal` | `inputs.goal` | |
| `experience` | `inputs.experience` | beginner clamp and beginner first-block peak |
| `nutritionPhase` | `inputs.nutritionPhase` | read only by `roles.js:91` for `'aggressive_cut'`, which the profile path never produces (P8) |
| `recoveryRating` | `inputs.recoveryRating` | changes the person's own clocks (order and reported readiness only, `planner.js:143-155`) |
| `focusMuscles` | `resolveWeakPointKeys(inputs.weakPoints.slice(0, 3))` | `planEngine.js:61-89`. Labels map to keys: Chest and Upper Chest to `chest`; Lats / Back Width and Back Thickness to `back`; Side Delts, Rear Delts, Front Delts, Biceps, Triceps, Quads, Hamstrings, Glutes, Adductors, Calves to their keys; Core / Abs to `abs`; Traps to `traps`. Duplicate keys are dropped after the slice (P13). |
| `addedMuscles` | `[]` | no profile field exists (`planAutoGen.js:1781-1783`) |
| `firstBlock` | `plannerV2FirstBlock(userId)` | |
| `learnedFactor`, `ownGaps` | `plannerV2Personalisation` | |
| `loggedWeekly` | `plannerV2LoggedWeekly(userId)` | `null` for a person with no training in the last 4 weeks |
| `choices` | catalogue with kept exercises | section 3 |
| `divisionMatrix` | `planEngine.DIVISION_MATRIX` | section 5.2 |
| `isStrength` | `inputs.phase === 'strength_size'` | rep ranges and rest only (section 7.4) |

Not passed: `phase` itself (so the `weak_point` phase has no effect on the new
planner; weak points become focus muscles in every phase, `roles.js:7-9`),
`age`, and the demonstrated structure.

### 2.3 Clamps and defaults inside `buildPlan` (`planner.js:108-155`)

- Sessions `n` = `clampDays(inputs)`: `max(2, min(6, round(daysPerWeek || 3)))`, then at most 4 for `experience === 'beginner'` (`planner.js:311-316`). Fixed mode uses the authored session count (2 to 6, else `RangeError`, `planner.js:334-338`).
- `focusMuscles` filtered to `PLAN_MUSCLES` (`planner.js:111`; `roles.js:39-44`).
- `goal` defaults to `'general'` (`planner.js:132`); `equipment` to `'full_gym'` (`:136`); `sessionLengthMinutes` to 60 when not positive (`:137`).
- `typical` = `TYPICAL_WEEK_GAP_HOURS[n]` (`planner.js:138`; `recovery/constants.js:689-697`).
- `ownGaps` used only when its length equals `n` (`planner.js:139`).
- `factor` = `learnedFactor` clamped to `[LEARNED_FACTOR.min 0.75, LEARNED_FACTOR.max 1.4]`, else `null` (`planner.js:127-129`; `science.js:205-212`).
- `lowestRir` = `min(BLOCK.rirLadder[0..4])` = 1 (`planner.js:142`; `science.js:140`).
- `hoursVolume` = the population clock (rating `average`, no factor) (`planner.js:146`, `154`); every structural decision uses it.
- `personal` = factor present or rating not `average` (`planner.js:155`); only then does `personalise` run.

A beginner who asks for 6 days gets a 4-session plan; `buildPlanInputs`
still reports 6 (`planAutoGen.js:124`). Observed by execution (probe in
section 11, P14).

---

## 3. The exercise catalogue (`src/lib/plan/catalogue.js`)

### 3.1 `resolveCatalogue({ library, profile, loggedExerciseNames })` (`catalogue.js:685-743`)

1. Kit = `profile`, or `'full_gym'` when empty (`:686`). The six kits are `CATALOGUE_PROFILES` (`:143-145`). An unknown kit matches no row (`profilesOf(row).includes(kit)` is false, `:702`), so every muscle resolves empty and `buildPlanWithPlannerV2` returns `null` (`planAutoGen.js:1779`): the legacy generator runs.
2. The library is indexed by name for catalogue names only; a built-in row beats a custom row of the same name, then the lower id (`indexLibrary`, `:608-621`).
3. For each muscle in `CATALOGUE_MUSCLES` order (`:136-141`), for each role in catalogue order (`:709`):
   - credited roles are skipped (`:710`);
   - `pick(names)`: names not already taken for this muscle, present in the library, with a load prescription (`exerciseType` `weight_reps` or `weighted_bodyweight`, `:599-602`) and carrying the kit in `equipmentProfiles` (`:698-704`). Among those, the first that is in `loggedExerciseNames`, else the first in catalogue order (`:705-706`). Recency of the logged list plays no part: the first logged name in catalogue order wins.
   - If none, and the role's rank is 1 or 2, the kit's `THIN_KIT[kit][muscle][roleId]` names are tried the same way; a pick from there is `thinKit: true` (`:713-719`).
   - The chosen row becomes `{ name, exerciseId, role, rank, kind, credits, direct: 1, primaryMuscle, reason, grade, source, thinKit, optIn, logged }` (`:723-738`).
4. `kind` = `paramKeyOf(row)` (`:573-580`): `isolation` when `compoundIsolation === 'isolation'`; else `heavy_compound` for equipment category `barbell` or `landmine`; else `machine` for `machine_selectorised` or `machine_plate_loaded`; else `mod_compound`.

### 3.2 Roles per muscle (`catalogue.js:167-477`)

| Muscle | Rank: role id: names in preference order |
|---|---|
| chest | 1 `flat_press`: Barbell Bench Press, Dumbbell Bench Press, Machine Chest Press. 2 `incline_press`: Incline Dumbbell Press, Incline Barbell Bench Press, Incline Machine Press. 3 `chest_fly`: Pec Deck (Machine Fly), Cable Crossover (High to Low) |
| back | 1 `vertical_pull`: Lat Pulldown (Wide Grip), Pull-Up, Chin-Up, Lat Pulldown (Neutral Grip). 2 `horizontal_row`: Seated Cable Row, Chest-Supported Row (Dumbbell), Machine Row (Chest Supported), Barbell Row (Bent Over), Dumbbell Row. 3 `other_pull`: all of the above in that order |
| side_delts | 1 `lateral_raise`: Dumbbell Lateral Raise. 2 `lateral_raise_alt`: Cable Lateral Raise, Machine Lateral Raise |
| rear_delts | 1 `reverse_fly`: Reverse Pec Deck. 2 `rear_delt_alt`: Face Pull, Dumbbell Rear Delt Fly |
| front_delts | 1 credited (`front_credit`). 2 `overhead_press`: Barbell Overhead Press, Dumbbell Shoulder Press, Machine Shoulder Press |
| traps | 1 `shrug`: Dumbbell Shrug, Barbell Shrug |
| biceps | 1 `preacher_curl`: EZ Bar Preacher Curl, Preacher Curl (Dumbbell), Preacher Curl Machine, Preacher Curl (Barbell). 2 `standing_curl`: Barbell Curl, Dumbbell Curl, Cable Curl. 3 `hammer_curl`: Hammer Curl |
| triceps | 1 `overhead_extension`: Cable Overhead Tricep Extension, Dumbbell Overhead Tricep Extension. 2 `pushdown`: Tricep Pushdown (Rope), Tricep Pushdown (Bar). 3 `close_grip_press`: Close-Grip Bench Press |
| quads | 1 `squat_or_press`: Barbell Back Squat, Hack Squat Machine, Leg Press. 2 `leg_extension`: Leg Extension. 3 `other_squat`: Leg Press, Hack Squat Machine, Barbell Back Squat. 4 `lunge`: Walking Lunge, Bulgarian Split Squat |
| hamstrings | 1 `leg_curl`: Seated Leg Curl, Lying Leg Curl. 2 `hip_hinge`: Romanian Deadlift (Barbell), Romanian Deadlift (Dumbbell) |
| glutes | 1 credited (`glute_credit`). 2 `hip_thrust`: Barbell Hip Thrust, Machine Hip Thrust. 3 `other_hip_thrust`: Machine Hip Thrust, Barbell Hip Thrust. 4 `glute_kickback`: Glute Kickback Machine, Cable Kickback. 5 `glute_bridge`: Barbell Glute Bridge, Dumbbell Glute Bridge, Glute Bridge |
| calves | 1 `standing_calf`: Standing Calf Raise (Machine). 2 `seated_calf`: Seated Calf Raise, Seated Machine Calf Raise. 3 `leg_press_calf`: Leg Press Calf Raise |
| abs | 1 `loaded_crunch`: Cable Crunch, Machine Crunch. 2 `hanging_raise`: Hanging Knee Raise, Hanging Leg Raise |
| forearms (opt-in flag) | 1 `wrist_curl`: Barbell Wrist Curl, Dumbbell Wrist Curl, Cable Wrist Curl, Band Wrist Curl |
| adductors (opt-in flag) | 1 credited. 2 `adduction_machine`: Hip Adduction Machine |
| neck (opt-in flag) | 1 `neck_machine`: Neck Flexion (Machine), Neck Extension (Machine), Plate Neck Curl, Neck Curl |
| tibialis (opt-in flag) | 1 `tibialis_raise`: Tibialis Raise (Wall), Seated Tibialis Raise, Dumbbell Tibialis Raise (Seated) |

The `optIn` flag is carried on the choice (`:736`) but `resolveCatalogue`
returns these muscles' choices for every person; whether they are trained is
decided by the roles (section 4), not by the catalogue.

### 3.3 Thin kits (`catalogue.js:486-545`)

Used only for a rank 1 or 2 role with no listed name for the kit
(`:713`). `full_gym` has none. `machines_cables`: traps shrug (Cable Shrug,
Machine Shrug), hamstrings hinge (Smith Machine Romanian Deadlift).
`dumbbells_only` and `home_gym` share `DUMBBELL_KIT` (`:486-492`): triceps
pushdown (Dumbbell Skull Crusher), quads squat (Goblet Squat) and leg
extension (Bulgarian Split Squat), glutes hip thrust (Dumbbell Hip Thrust,
Dumbbell Glute Bridge), calves (Dumbbell Calf Raise (Standing); Seated
Dumbbell Calf Raise), abs (Decline Crunch, Weighted Dumbbell Crunch).
`barbell_plates` (`:510-518`) and `bodyweight` (`:519-544`) have their own
lists. A thin-kit pick reads `thinKitReason`, grade `D` (`:553-555`, `732-734`).

"Thin kit" (a catalogue fallback) is a different thing from the allocator's
"thin equipment" (a muscle with exactly one choice, section 6.6.2). The second
fires in a full gym for every muscle the catalogue gives one non-credited role:
front delts, traps, forearms, adductors, neck and tibialis (observed, P7).

### 3.4 Credits (`catalogue.js:97-109`, `649`, `663-673`)

Credits are curated per role, never the corpus's secondary muscles. At 0.5 a
set: flat and incline presses credit triceps and front delts; the overhead
press triceps and side delts; vertical pulls biceps (the wide-grip pulldown
also rear delts); rows biceps, rear delts and traps; squat, hack squat and leg
press glutes and adductors; hinges glutes; hip thrusts and bridges hamstrings;
lunges and split squats glutes; the close-grip bench press (and thin-kit dips
and diamond push-ups listed under triceps) chest and front delts. Isolation
rows credit nothing. `creditsByName` overrides the role's credits for named
exercises (`:151-160`, `204-206`, `289`, `295`, `312`, `318-320`).
`catalogueCredits(muscle, name)` returns the credits of the role of that
muscle that lists the name (or lists it as a thin-kit name), else `{}`
(`:663-673`).

### 3.5 Continuity of the person's own exercises: `plannerV2KeptChoices` (`planAutoGen.js:1501-1569`)

Input: the catalogue's choices, the kept rows from `plannerV2KeptExercises`
(in the person's plan order) and `rowOf` (choice to library row).

1. Each kept row is skipped if it has no name, a name already seen, an `exerciseType` other than `weight_reps` or `weighted_bodyweight`, or a primary muscle with no catalogue key; a primary muscle of `shoulders` is read as `side_delts` (`:1509-1516`).
2. The kept choice is `{ name, exerciseId, role: null, rank: 1, kind: deriveParamKey(category, compoundIsolation), credits: catalogueCredits(muscle, name), direct: 1, primaryMuscle, thinKit: false, logged: true, kept: true, ... }` (`:1519-1541`). A kept exercise the muscle's catalogue does not list has no credits.
3. Per muscle (`:1545-1567`): the same name already in the list keeps its place; else the first unclaimed catalogue choice of the same family (`slotKey(muscle, movementFamily(...))`) is replaced, keeping that place's `role` and `rank`; else, if the family exists but every place is claimed, it goes right after the family's last place; else it goes to the FRONT of the muscle's list, in plan order.
4. With nothing kept, the catalogue's own object is returned (`:1543`).

Because the allocator takes a muscle's choices in list order (section 6.6.2),
a kept exercise at the front of a list displaces the catalogue's first choice
from the plan whenever the muscle is trained with fewer exercises than the
list holds.

## 4. Roles (`src/lib/plan/roles.js`, `divisionStandard.js`, `coachingGoals.js`)

### 4.1 `assignRoles` (`roles.js:80-157`)

Constants: `PLAN_MUSCLES` (17 keys, `:39-44`); `GROWTH_MUSCLES` = chest,
back, side_delts, rear_delts, biceps, triceps, quads, hamstrings, glutes,
calves, abs (`:47-50`); `INDIRECT_MUSCLES` = front_delts, traps, forearms,
adductors (`:53`); `OPT_IN_MUSCLES` = neck, tibialis (`:56`);
`MAX_FOCUS_MUSCLES = 3` (`:58`); `MAINTENANCE_TARGET = { deEmphasised: 6,
indirect: 4 }` (`:61`).

- `cut` = 2 (`BLOCK.cutPhasePeakReduction`, `science.js:141`) when `nutritionPhase === 'aggressive_cut'`, else 0 (`:63`, `:91`).
- `standardPeak` = 14 (`ROLE_TARGETS.standard.firstBlockBeginnerPeak`) for a beginner in a first block, else 20 (`ROLE_TARGETS.standard.peakMax`) (`:92-94`; `science.js:97`).

For each muscle in `PLAN_MUSCLES` order:

| Case (first that applies) | role | weight | peak (fractional) | growthFloor | directFloor | direct | Lines |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| In focus list (first 3 distinct valid keys) | focus | 1.5 | 22 minus cut | 20 | division floor, else `STANDARD_DIRECT_FLOOR[m]`, else 4 | true | `:101-111` |
| Growth muscle, or overlay at least 1.0, or added; and trainable; overlay above 1.0 | standard | `min(1.5, overlay)` | `standardPeak` minus cut | 10 | as above | true | `:120-139` |
| As above, overlay exactly 1.0 or absent | standard | 1.0 | `standardPeak` minus cut | 10 | as above | true | same |
| As above, overlay below 1.0 ("de-emphasised") | standard | 0 | `max(directFloor, 6)` minus `min(cut, 2)` | `min(10, directFloor)` | as above | true | `:131-136` |
| Opt-in (neck, tibialis) not focused or added | absent from the output | | | | | | `:143` |
| Anything else (front delts, traps, forearms, adductors not judged) | maintenance | 0 | 4 | 0 | 0 | false | `:147-154` |

`trainedMuscles` is only set in fixed mode (`planner.js:124`); for a generated
plan every muscle can train (`:85`, `:99`).

The file header says a division's de-emphasised muscles become maintenance at
6 (`roles.js:15-17`); the code makes them standard with weight 0 (`:113-139`).
Listed in section 11 (P12).

Standard direct floors (`science.js:116-132`): chest 10, back 12, quads 10,
hamstrings 8, glutes 8, calves 8, biceps 8, triceps 8, side_delts 8,
rear_delts 6, abs 6, traps 4, adductors 4, forearms 4; anything else 4
(`STANDARD_DIRECT_FLOOR_DEFAULT`).

Consequences by the table in 4.1:

- A growth muscle with an overlay below 1.0 is "de-emphasised": standard, weight 0, low peak. A non-growth muscle (traps, front delts, forearms, adductors) with an overlay below 1.0, or with none, is maintenance and gets no exercise of its own (`direct: false`).
- A non-growth muscle with an overlay of 1.0 or more is trained for growth: front delts in mens_physique and bodybuilding; traps in bodybuilding, womens_physique (1.00) and womens_bodybuilding; forearms in bodybuilding; adductors in bikini, wellness, figure, womens_physique and womens_bodybuilding.
- `PHASE_OVERLAYS` (`coachingGoals.js:610-616`) is not read by the new planner (grep of `src/lib/plan/`: no match).

## 5. Week structures

### 5.1 General families (`families.js:42-87`)

Muscle sets (`families.js:22-28`): UPPER = chest, back, side_delts,
rear_delts, front_delts, traps, biceps, triceps, forearms, neck. LOWER =
quads, hamstrings, glutes, adductors, calves, abs, tibialis. PUSH = chest,
side_delts, front_delts, triceps, abs. PULL = back, rear_delts, traps, biceps,
forearms, abs, neck. LEGS = LOWER. ARMS_SHOULDERS = biceps, triceps,
side_delts, rear_delts, front_delts, forearms, abs. FULL = UPPER + LOWER.

| Days | key (recognisable) | Sessions (muscle set) |
|---|---|---|
| 2 | `full_body_ab` (0) | Full Body A (FULL), Full Body B (FULL) |
| 2 | `upper_lower` (0) | Upper (UPPER), Lower (LOWER) |
| 3 | `ppl` (0) | Push (PUSH), Pull (PULL), Legs (LEGS) |
| 3 | `full_body_abc` (0) | Full Body A, B, C (FULL) |
| 3 | `upper_lower_full` (1) | Upper, Lower, Full Body |
| 4 | `upper_lower_x2` (0) | Upper A, Lower A, Upper B, Lower B |
| 4 | `ppl_upper` (1) | Push, Pull, Legs, Upper |
| 4 | `ppl_arms` (1) | Push, Pull, Legs, Arms and Shoulders (ARMS_SHOULDERS) |
| 5 | `ppl_upper_lower` (0) | Push, Pull, Legs, Upper, Lower |
| 5 | `upper_lower_x2_full` (1) | Upper A, Lower A, Upper B, Lower B, Full Body |
| 5 | `upper_lower_x2_focus` (2, only with a focus muscle) | Upper A, Lower A, Upper B, Lower B, Focus (the focus muscles, then ARMS_SHOULDERS not already listed) |
| 5 | `body_part` (1) | Chest and Triceps (chest, triceps, front_delts); Back and Biceps (back, biceps, rear_delts, traps, forearms); Quads and Calves (quads, calves, adductors, tibialis); Shoulders and Abs (side_delts, front_delts, rear_delts, traps, abs, neck); Hamstrings and Glutes (hamstrings, glutes, adductors, abs) |
| 6 | `ppl_x2` (0) | Push A, Pull A, Legs A, Push B, Pull B, Legs B |
| 6 | `upper_lower_x3` (1) | Upper A, Lower A, Upper B, Lower B, Upper C, Lower C |

`familiesFor(n, { focusMuscles })` (`families.js:97-113`) clamps n to 2 to 6,
drops `needsFocus` families without a focus muscle, and fills the Focus
session's list.
