Helms on set counting: "Sets count as full sets for primary muscles and half sets for secondary muscles" (page, as returned by the fetch tool), the opposite of the Part A survey, which has no fractional rule.

### B4. Modal structures by days per week, with per-muscle exposure

Sample bodybuilding n = 7 (B2); sample general n = 6 programmes with a day layout (B3). "Where sources disagree" uses only what the pages show.

**Open bodybuilding (pro and coach splits)**

| Days | Modal structure in sample | Legs / week | Chest, back, shoulders, arms / week | Disagreement |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 3 | NOT FOUND in any named pro split | n/a | n/a | n/a |
| 4 | Body-part split, one major group per day (Yates 4-day: chest + biceps, back, triceps + shoulders, legs; Meadows 4-day: legs, chest + shoulders, back, arms). 2 of 2 give legs once | 1 | Each 1 | Yates sources differ on days (Generation Iron: 4 days, "frequency not stated"; Set for Set: 4 training days on a 7-day cycle, abs 2x) |
| 5 | Two sessions of legs and a body-part split for the rest (Walker 5-day; Meadows Jacked Gorilla). 2 of 2 give legs twice | 2 | Mixed: Walker chest 2, Meadows each 1 | Walker BarBend says chest 2, legs 2, others "for performance and recovery"; Fitness Volt version has back 2, biceps 3. Meadows source says each group trained every 4 to 6 days while listing a 1x layout; the page flags its own inconsistency |
| 6 | One or two muscles a day, each group once (Lunsford, Sarcev, Rambod arms plan). Legs split across two days in Lunsford and Sarcev; Rambod 1 | 1 to 2 | Each 1 (calves 2; abs 2 in Lunsford) | Lunsford's full-week layout is Yardbarker's; Drworkout and Fitness Phantom (not fetched) were reported by search as slightly different |

**General hypertrophy**

| Days | Modal structure | Legs / week | Upper per muscle / week | Disagreement |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 3 | Full body x3 (Helms) or Lower / Push / Pull (Helms, 1x) | 3 or 1 | 3 or 1 | Helms lists both |
| 4 | Upper / Lower / Upper / Lower (Helms, Nippard Fundamentals, RP, BodySpec: 4 of 4 sources name it). Alternatives: Full Push / Full Pull x2 (Helms), Nippard 4x Ultimate PPL (legs, push, pull, full body) | 2 | 2 | Nippard 4x PPL page implies about 1 plus a full-body day |
| 5 | Push / Pull / Legs + Upper + Lower (Nippard PPLUL, RP PPL 5x) or Upper / Lower / Push / Pull / Legs (Nippard ULPPL). Helms: Lower / Push / Pull / Full / Full | 2 (PPLUL, ULPPL) | 2 (push/pull muscles, via the Upper day) | Helms also lists Chest / Back / Lower / Shoulders / Arms at 1x |
| 6 | Push / Pull / Legs x2 (Nippard 6x). Helms: Lower / Push / Pull x2 (2x), Full Push / Full Pull x3 (3x), Full Body x6 | 2 (PPL x2) | 2 (PPL x2) | Helms spans 2x to 6x |

Observed split between camps: all open-bodybuilding splits that give a frequency place chest, back, shoulders and arms at 1x (apart from Walker at 2x chest), while all general-hypertrophy sources place the same muscles at 2x or more at 4 to 6 days. The one-per-week pro vs two-per-week general contrast is directly visible in the tables above; no source I fetched argues why. Helms (2x to 4x recommended) and Yates (once every ~6 days, per Set for Set) disagree on frequency in print.

### B5. Weekly direct-set ranges from sources

| Source and muscle | Value | URL |
|---|---|---|
| Helms, hypertrophy, any muscle | minimum ~4, practical 10 to 20, max ~30; 4 to 10 sets: 1-2x; 11 to 20: 2-3x; 21 to 30: 3+x | https://rippedbody.com/how-to-build-training-programs/ |
| Stronger By Science, general | "most people ... probably still come out to <15 sets per muscle group per week"; hypertrophy volume camps: 10 or fewer, 10 to 20, more than 20 (fractional sets near failure) | https://www.strongerbyscience.com/volume/ |
| RP (BodySpec) hypertrophy phase | 10 to 12 sets per muscle; mesocycle 12, 14, 16 | https://www.bodyspec.com/blog/post/renaissance_periodization_principles_and_guide |
| Israetel, chest | MV 8, MEV 10, MAV 12-20, MRV 22; 1.5-3x/week | https://drmikeisraetel.com/dr-mike-israetel-mv-mev-mav-mrv-explained/ |
| Israetel, back | MV 8, MEV 14-22, MAV ~25; 2-4x | same |
| Israetel, biceps | MV 4-6, MEV 14-20, MAV 20-26; 2-6x | same |
| Israetel, calves | MV 6, MEV 8, MAV 12-16, MRV 20; 2-4x | same |
| Israetel, abs | MV 0, MEV 4-10 (16-20 if doing a lot), MAV ~25; 3-5x | same |

RP 2024 per-muscle landmarks (MEV to MRV, with the 2017 values and the 6-day programme's week 1 to week 4 sets) from Lift Vault (https://liftvault.com/programs/bodybuilding/mike-israetel-5-week-hypertrophy-workout-routine-spreadsheet/):

| Muscle | 2017 MEV / MRV | 2024 MEV / MRV | Programme week 1 to 4 |
|---|---|---|---|
| Chest | 10 / 22+ | 4-6 / 16-24 | 10 to 16 |
| Back | 10 / 25+ | 6-8 / 20-26 | 10 to 20 |
| Quads | 8 / 20+ | 4-6 / 14-18 | 8 to 14 |
| Hamstrings | 6 / 20+ | 2-4 / 8-14 | 4 to 8 |
| Glutes | 0 / 16+ | 6-8 / 24-30 | 8 to 12 |
| Biceps | 8 / 26+ | 8-10 / 20-26 | 10 to 18 |
| Triceps | 6 / 18+ | 4-6 / 16-20 | 8 to 16 |
| Side delts | 8* / 26+* | 6-8 / 24-30 | 10 to 20 |
| Rear delts | 8* / 26+* | 0-4 / 12-20 | 4 to 10 |
| Front delts | 0 / 12+ | 0-2 / 8-12 | 3 to 4 |
| Calves | 8 / 20+ | 4-6 / 16-24 | 8 to 14 |
| Abs | 0 / 25+ | 0-4 / 12-20 | 4 to 10 |
| Traps | 0 / 26+ | 0-4 / 12-20 | 4 to 10 |

Note: the Israetel page and the Lift Vault page use different generations of landmarks (2017 style vs 2024), and Lift Vault is a third-party spreadsheet seller.

Pro-athlete set counts from the splits pages (exercises x sets as the page listed them): Meadows legs 24 + 16 = 40 per week (Jacked Gorilla tally); Yates back 4 x 5 exercises plus rack pulls (about 19 to 22) per week from a single session per week (Generation Iron lists the exercises; the sum is mine); Walker push day shoulders and arms 3 + 3 + 5 + 4 + 5 (one session). Part A's median chest/back/delt sets of 20 to 33 per week for men sit at or above every general-source range except Helms's upper end (30), which is an observed gap, not an explained one.

### B6. What these programmes do NOT do (cited)

| Claim | Source and quote | URL |
|---|---|---|
| A split does not specify weekly volume | "does not by itself specify weekly volume" (SetGraph on Nippard splits) | https://setgraph.app/articles/jeff-nippard-split-explained-best-ulppl-pplul-upper-lower-programs-ranked |
| No per-muscle weekly sets given for Nippard programmes on the public pages | Product page lists "Exact warmups, sets, reps, rest periods, and RPE" only inside the paid product | https://jeffnippard.com/products/the-ultimate-push-pull-legs-system |
| Pro splits are not the athlete's exact routine | "This post is not John Meadows' exact workout routine" | https://jackedgorilla.com/john-meadows-workout-routine/ |
| Meadows source is internally inconsistent about frequency | Page says each muscle every 4 to 6 days but lists a 1x/week layout and calls a five-day layout "4-day" | https://jackedgorilla.com/john-meadows-workout-routine/ |
| Yates pages disagree on frequency and set definitions | Set for Set: "Only the last set is an all-out brutal set"; Generation Iron: 3 to 4 sets per exercise with no frequency stated | https://www.setforset.com/blogs/news/dorian-yates-blood-and-guts , https://generationiron.com/dorian-yates-blood-guts-workout/ |
| Lunsford has no published full split | Search result stated he "has never officially published a complete breakdown"; fetched Yardbarker page lists days but "Sets/exercises: None are stated" | https://www.yardbarker.com/lifestyle/articles/mr_olympia_derek_lunsford_shares_how_to_train_for_summer_physique_in_60_days/s1_17782_43903716 |
| Walker's non-priority muscles are not trained for growth | "mainly for performance and recovery" (as returned by the fetch tool) | https://barbend.com/news/nick-walker-5-day-training-split/ |
| Fractional or indirect sets are an input some sources use and some ignore | Helms: half-set credit for secondary muscles; Part A survey: no fractional rule stated | https://rippedbody.com/how-to-build-training-programs/ |
| Most people will not reach high volume | "your training volume is more-or-less dictated by your life outside of the gym" (SBS) | https://www.strongerbyscience.com/volume/ |
| Pro and general programmes do not state a division, sex or body-type adjustment | No page I fetched states one (NOT FOUND) | n/a |
| No page I fetched ties a split to the judging criteria | NOT FOUND | n/a |

### B7. Gaps and risks for use

- NOT FOUND: any 3-day open-bodybuilding split from a named pro or coach; Aceto, Rambod (own five-day), Henselmans (page-level), Nuckols (programme-level) and RP "off-season" template with muscles per day.
- Fetch limits: muscleandstrength.com, muscleandbrawn.com, strongerbyscience frequency and mennohenselmans.com intermittently returned 403; only the SBS volume page was parsed from raw HTML.
- Three of the seven pro splits are media or fan write-ups, not the athlete's publication. Treat them as descriptions of a style, not an athlete's verified programme.
- Part A is a 2020 self-report with n = 3 in two divisions and no fractional-set rule; its figures are descriptive of practice, not a dose recommendation.
